  26\tdef add_book(database: Database, title: str, author: str) -> BookRow:\n    27\t    \"\"\"Store a book appended after every existing book and return the stored row.\n    28\t\n    29\t    Values BY clause.\n    44\t    \"\"\"\n    45\t    return sorted(database.books.list(), key=lambda row: row.id)\n    46\t\n    47\t\n    48\tdef remove_book(database: Database, book_id: int) -> None:\n
